        (function () {
            if (window.__SP_CONSENT_GATE__) return;
            window.__SP_CONSENT_GATE__ = true;

            const consentCategoryMap = Object.assign({
                analytics: ['C0002'],
                advertising: ['C0004'],
                // GTM may carry both analytics and ad tags; allow when either C0002 or C0004 is active.
                gtm: ['C0002', 'C0004']
            }, window.__SP_CONSENT_CATEGORY_MAP__ || {});

            const blockedHostRules = [
                { matcher: 'googletagmanager.com', categories: ['gtm'] },
                { matcher: 'googletagservices.com', categories: ['advertising'] },
                { matcher: 'doubleclick.net', categories: ['advertising'] },
                { matcher: 'googlesyndication.com', categories: ['advertising'] },
                { matcher: 'google-analytics.com', categories: ['analytics'] },
                { matcher: 'analytics.google.com', categories: ['analytics'] },
                { matcher: 'imasdk.googleapis.com', categories: ['advertising'] },
                { matcher: 'partnerpixels', categories: ['advertising'] },
                { matcher: 'sdk.mvp.fan', categories: ['advertising'] }
            ];

            const queuedNodes = [];
            const gtmContainerId = (window.__SP_GTM_CONTAINER_ID__ || 'GTM-PM6PK4FK').toString().trim();
            /** GTM must not run until OneTrust invokes OptanonWrapper (initial load or consent change). */
            let spOptanonWrapperHasRun = false;

            function debugLog(action, payload) {
                if (!window.__SP_CONSENT_DEBUG__) return;
                try {
                    console.log('[SP Consent Gate]', action, payload || {});
                } catch (e) {}
            }

            function getConsentGroups() {
                const raw = (window.OnetrustActiveGroups || '').toString();
                return raw.split(',').map(function (g) { return g.trim(); }).filter(Boolean);
            }

            function hasConsentForCategoryKey(categoryKey, activeGroups) {
                const mappedGroups = consentCategoryMap[categoryKey] || [];
                if (!mappedGroups.length) return false;
                return mappedGroups.some(function (groupId) {
                    return activeGroups.indexOf(groupId) !== -1;
                });
            }

            function hasConsentForCategories(categoryKeys) {
                const activeGroups = getConsentGroups();
                if (!activeGroups.length) return false;
                return categoryKeys.every(function (categoryKey) {
                    return hasConsentForCategoryKey(categoryKey, activeGroups);
                });
            }

            function hasConsentForAnyCategory(categoryKeys) {
                const activeGroups = getConsentGroups();
                if (!activeGroups.length) return false;
                return categoryKeys.some(function (categoryKey) {
                    return hasConsentForCategoryKey(categoryKey, activeGroups);
                });
            }

            function normalizeUrl(value) {
                if (!value || typeof value !== 'string') return '';
                try {
                    return new URL(value, window.location.origin).href;
                } catch (e) {
                    return value;
                }
            }

            function getBlockedRule(url) {
                const normalized = normalizeUrl(url).toLowerCase();
                if (!normalized) return null;
                return blockedHostRules.find(function (rule) {
                    return normalized.indexOf(rule.matcher) !== -1;
                });
            }

            function rememberNode(node, src, categories) {
                queuedNodes.push({
                    tag: node.tagName,
                    src: src,
                    categories: categories || [],
                    type: node.type || '',
                    async: !!node.async,
                    defer: !!node.defer,
                    crossOrigin: node.crossOrigin || '',
                    referrerPolicy: node.referrerPolicy || ''
                });
            }

            function replayQueuedNodes() {
                if (!queuedNodes.length) return;
                const stillBlocked = [];
                while (queuedNodes.length) {
                    const item = queuedNodes.shift();
                    if (!hasConsentForCategories(item.categories)) {
                        stillBlocked.push(item);
                        continue;
                    }

                    const el = document.createElement(item.tag);
                    if (item.tag === 'SCRIPT' && item.type) el.type = item.type;
                    if (item.tag === 'SCRIPT') {
                        el.async = item.async;
                        el.defer = item.defer;
                    }
                    if (item.crossOrigin) el.crossOrigin = item.crossOrigin;
                    if (item.referrerPolicy) el.referrerPolicy = item.referrerPolicy;
                    el.src = item.src;
                    (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(el);
                    debugLog('replayed', { tag: item.tag, src: item.src, categories: item.categories });
                }

                if (stillBlocked.length) {
                    stillBlocked.forEach(function (item) { queuedNodes.push(item); });
                }
            }

            function activateConsentIframes() {
                const iframes = document.querySelectorAll('iframe[data-sp-consent-src][data-sp-consent-category]');
                if (!iframes.length) return;

                iframes.forEach(function (iframe) {
                    const category = (iframe.getAttribute('data-sp-consent-category') || '').trim();
                    if (!category) return;
                    if (!hasConsentForCategories([category])) return;

                    const realSrc = iframe.getAttribute('data-sp-consent-src');
                    if (!realSrc) return;
                    if (iframe.getAttribute('src') === realSrc) return;

                    iframe.setAttribute('src', realSrc);
                    debugLog('iframe-activated', { src: realSrc, category: category });
                });
            }

            function activateConsentScripts() {
                const scripts = document.querySelectorAll('script[data-sp-consent-src][data-sp-consent-category]');
                if (!scripts.length) return;

                scripts.forEach(function (scriptEl) {
                    const category = (scriptEl.getAttribute('data-sp-consent-category') || '').trim();
                    if (!category) return;
                    if (!hasConsentForCategories([category])) return;

                    const realSrc = scriptEl.getAttribute('data-sp-consent-src');
                    if (!realSrc) return;
                    if (scriptEl.dataset.spConsentLoaded === '1') return;

                    const s = document.createElement('script');
                    s.src = realSrc;
                    s.async = true;
                    (document.head || document.documentElement).appendChild(s);
                    scriptEl.dataset.spConsentLoaded = '1';
                    debugLog('script-activated', { src: realSrc, category: category });
                });
            }

            function loadGtmIfConsented() {
                if (!gtmContainerId) return;
                if (!spOptanonWrapperHasRun) return;
                if (!hasConsentForCategories(['gtm'])) return;
                if (window.__SP_GTM_LOADED__) return;
                if (document.querySelector('script[src*="googletagmanager.com/gtm.js?id=' + gtmContainerId + '"]')) {
                    window.__SP_GTM_LOADED__ = true;
                    return;
                }

                window.dataLayer = window.dataLayer || [];
                window.dataLayer.push({ 'gtm.start': new Date().getTime(), event: 'gtm.js' });

                const gtmScript = document.createElement('script');
                gtmScript.async = true;
                gtmScript.src = 'https://www.googletagmanager.com/gtm.js?id=' + encodeURIComponent(gtmContainerId);
                const head = document.head || document.documentElement;
                // Bypass patched appendChild so gtm.js is not re-queued under legacy dual-category rules.
                appendChildNative.call(head, gtmScript);
                window.__SP_GTM_LOADED__ = true;
                debugLog('gtm-loaded', { containerId: gtmContainerId, activeGroups: getConsentGroups() });
            }

            function shouldBlockNode(node) {
                if (!node || !node.tagName) return false;
                const tag = node.tagName.toUpperCase();
                if (tag !== 'SCRIPT' && tag !== 'IFRAME' && tag !== 'IMG') return false;
                const src = node.src || node.getAttribute && node.getAttribute('src') || '';
                const rule = getBlockedRule(src);
                if (!rule) return false;
                return !hasConsentForCategories(rule.categories);
            }

            const appendChildNative = Element.prototype.appendChild;
            const insertBeforeNative = Element.prototype.insertBefore;

            Element.prototype.appendChild = function (node) {
                if (shouldBlockNode(node)) {
                    const src = node.src || node.getAttribute('src') || '';
                    const rule = getBlockedRule(src);
                    const categories = rule ? rule.categories : [];
                    rememberNode(node, src, categories);
                    debugLog('blocked', { method: 'appendChild', tag: node.tagName, src: src, categories: categories });
                    return node;
                }
                return appendChildNative.call(this, node);
            };

            Element.prototype.insertBefore = function (newNode, referenceNode) {
                if (shouldBlockNode(newNode)) {
                    const src = newNode.src || newNode.getAttribute('src') || '';
                    const rule = getBlockedRule(src);
                    const categories = rule ? rule.categories : [];
                    rememberNode(newNode, src, categories);
                    debugLog('blocked', { method: 'insertBefore', tag: newNode.tagName, src: src, categories: categories });
                    return newNode;
                }
                return insertBeforeNative.call(this, newNode, referenceNode);
            };

            document.addEventListener('OneTrustGroupsUpdated', function () {
                debugLog('consent-updated', { activeGroups: getConsentGroups() });
                replayQueuedNodes();
                activateConsentIframes();
                activateConsentScripts();
                loadGtmIfConsented();
            });

            window.addEventListener('load', function () {
                debugLog('page-load', { activeGroups: getConsentGroups() });
                replayQueuedNodes();
                activateConsentIframes();
                activateConsentScripts();
                // Do not load GTM here: OneTrust must run OptanonWrapper / fire OneTrustGroupsUpdated first
                // so consent reflects banner interaction and saved preferences.
            });

            document.addEventListener('SPOptanonWrapperCalled', function () {
                debugLog('optanon-wrapper-called', { activeGroups: getConsentGroups() });
                replayQueuedNodes();
                activateConsentIframes();
                activateConsentScripts();
                loadGtmIfConsented();
            });

            window.__spConsentOnOptanonWrapper = function () {
                spOptanonWrapperHasRun = true;
                replayQueuedNodes();
                activateConsentIframes();
                activateConsentScripts();
                loadGtmIfConsented();
            };

            debugLog('initialized', { categoryMap: consentCategoryMap });
        })();

function loadLocalizedImages(countryCode) {
  var localizedImageElements = document.querySelectorAll('[data-localized-image]');
  var code = (countryCode || '').toLowerCase();
  var i;

  for (i = 0; i < localizedImageElements.length; i++) {
    var element = localizedImageElements[i];
    var srcFallback = element.getAttribute('data-src-fallback');
    var altFallback = element.getAttribute('data-alt-fallback');
    var src = element.getAttribute('data-src-' + code);
    var alt = element.getAttribute('data-alt-' + code);

    if (src || srcFallback) {
      element.src = src || srcFallback;
    }
    if (alt || altFallback) {
      element.alt = alt || altFallback;
    }

    element.setAttribute('data-localized-image', 'loaded');
  }
}

    .catch(function (error) {
      console.error('Error fetching geolocation:', error);
      loadLocalizedImages(null);
    });
})();

                var isAuth = false;
                var teamLogo = '';
                var teamId = '';

                /**
                 * Read the fan off the contract, if it is there and has settled.
                 *
                 * `state()` answers null until the SDK settles, and null is NOT signed out -- so an
                 * unsettled read leaves the skeleton alone rather than asserting anything.
                 */
                function readFan() {
                    var api = window.urcAuth;
                    var state = api && typeof api.state === 'function' ? api.state() : null;

                    isAuth = !!(state && state.signedIn);
                    teamId = isAuth && state.clubId ? String(state.clubId) : '';
                    teamLogo = teamId && crests[teamId] ? crests[teamId] : '';

                /*
                 * The emblem's inline style, which has to be written by JS because
                 * `.auth0-generic-emblem` in _header.scss carries only a border-radius -- the
                 * sizing, padding and cyan tile are all inline. **The same declaration is in the
                 * Blade markup for both buttons above**; if you change one, change all three.
                 */
                var EMBLEM_STYLE = 'width: 27px; height: 27px; background-color: #00cde6; padding: 4px; border-radius: 8px 0;';

                /*
                 * The crest's, which is the emblem's WITHOUT a background. The club colour comes
                 * from `_header.scss`'s `$team-logo-bg-colors` map keyed on `data-team-id`, and an
                 * inline `background-color` beats that wherever the map's `!important` does not
                 * reach -- everything in that file compiles under `.banner .navbar`, and one of
                 * these two buttons sits outside it. The sizing still has to be inline, because
                 * `.auth0-team-logo` sets no width or height either.
                 */
                var CREST_STYLE = 'width: 27px; height: 27px; padding: 4px; border-radius: 8px 0;';

                /**
                 * Turn one `img` into the generic emblem, completely.
                 *
                 * Both branches go through this so the two directions cannot drift apart, which is
                 * exactly what had happened: the signed-in branch wrote a crest and had no way
                 * back, so a fan with no club kept the PREVIOUS fan's crest.
                 */
                function writeEmblem(img) {
                    img.src = emblem;
                    img.alt = 'URC Logo';
                    img.className = 'auth0-generic-emblem';
                    img.removeAttribute('data-team-id');
                    img.setAttribute('style', EMBLEM_STYLE);
                }

                /**
                 * Turn one `img` into this fan's club crest.
                 *
                 * `data-team-id` is what selects the club's tile colour. applyAccountRow() has set
                 * it since the crest row was added; this half did not, so the button crest always
                 * fell through to the default slate.
                 */
                function writeCrest(img) {
                    img.src = teamLogo;
                    img.alt = 'Team Logo';
                    img.className = 'auth0-team-logo';
                    img.setAttribute('data-team-id', teamId);
                    img.setAttribute('style', CREST_STYLE);
                }

                function applyMenuItems() {
                document.querySelectorAll('.auth0-menu-item a').forEach(function (link) {
                    var span = link.querySelector('.header-sign-in');
                    var isMobile = !!link.closest('.mobile-signin');
                    if (isAuth) {
                        link.href = myAccountUrl;
                        link.classList.add('auth0-authenticated');
                        if (span) {
                            span.textContent = 'My Account';
                        } else if (!link.querySelector('img.auth0-team-logo')) {
                            span = document.createElement('span');
                            span.className = 'header-sign-in';
                            span.textContent = 'My Account';
                            link.insertBefore(span, link.firstChild);
                        }
                        /*
                         * A COMPLETE state, both ways. `teamLogo` is empty for a fan who has
                         * selected no club -- `team_name` is the empty string on a real dev-tenant
                         * fan -- and this used to be `if (teamLogo) { … }` with no else, so the
                         * crest branch could not undo itself. A fan with no club then kept
                         * whichever club crest the document had already been shown, under a
                         * `My URC` label, with no navigation needed: the auth bundle re-resolves on
                         * focus, visibilitychange, pageshow and the cross-tab broadcast.
                         */
                        var logoImg = link.querySelector('img');
                        if (logoImg) {
                            if (teamLogo) {
                                writeCrest(logoImg);
                            } else {
                                writeEmblem(logoImg);
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        link.href = signInUrl;
                        link.classList.remove('auth0-authenticated');
                        if (!span) {
                            span = document.createElement('span');
                            span.className = 'header-sign-in';
                            link.insertBefore(span, link.firstChild);
                        }
                        span.textContent = isMobile ? 'Sign in / Register' : 'Sign in/Register';
                        var imgs = link.querySelectorAll('img');
                        if (imgs.length === 0) {
                            var img = document.createElement('img');
                            writeEmblem(img);
                            link.appendChild(img);
                        } else {
                            imgs.forEach(writeEmblem);
                        }
                    }
                });
                }

                /*
                 * The mobile menu's account row. It carries the same state as the button above and
                 * is reached by none of the selectors that correct it: `.account-link` deliberately
                 * contains neither `auth0`, `sign-in` nor `signin`, so this loop selects the
                 * attribute instead and rebuilds the icon under this row's own class names. Keeping
                 * those class names clear of those three substrings is still the rule -- see the
                 * block comment further down the file.
                 *
                 * **The ordering argument this comment used to make is retired.** It explained why
                 * this half waited for DOMContentLoaded while the button loop above ran during
                 * parse: the row sits ~8KB below this script and does not exist yet, and deferring
                 * the button loop as well would have put the view's rewrite after
                 * `auth0-login.php`'s two `wp_footer` rewriters, reversing an order three scripts
                 * depended on. Both of those rewriters are deleted, and this whole reconcile is
                 * asynchronous now -- it waits for the SDK either way -- so there is no ordering
                 * left to protect. The row still has to exist before it can be written to, which is
                 * what the readyState branch at the bottom is for.
                 */
                function applyAccountRow() {
                    document.querySelectorAll('[data-account-row="identity"]').forEach(function (link) {
                        var label = link.querySelector('.account-link-label');
                        var icon = link.querySelector('.account-link-icon');
                        var crest = isAuth && teamLogo;
                        if (icon) {
                            icon.src = crest ? teamLogo : emblem;
                            icon.className = 'account-link-icon ' + (crest ? 'account-link-crest' : 'account-link-emblem');
                            /*
                             * The per-club crest background in _header.scss is an attribute selector,
                             * so a crest with no data-team-id reads as the default slate rather than
                             * the club colour.
                             */
                            if (crest && teamId) {
                                icon.setAttribute('data-team-id', teamId);
                            } else {
                                icon.removeAttribute('data-team-id');
                            }
                        }
                        if (label) {
                            label.textContent = isAuth ? 'My Account' : 'Sign in / Register';
                        }
                        link.href = isAuth ? myAccountUrl : signInUrl;
                    });
                }
                /**
                 * Write the whole state, both halves, from whatever `readFan()` last established.
                 *
                 * Both loops are idempotent and both write a COMPLETE state in either direction, so
                 * calling this on every announcement is what makes a sign-out landing while the page
                 * is open correct itself rather than needing its own path.
                 */
                function apply() {
                    readFan();
                    applyMenuItems();

                    // The row is ~8KB further down this document and a classic inline script runs
                    // while the parser is still there, so it may not exist on the first call. Both
                    // calls are made: this one, so it still works if the markup ever moves up, and
                    // one on DOMContentLoaded, which is what actually finds it today.
                    applyAccountRow();
                }

                apply();

                if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
                    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', apply);
                }

                /*
                 * Wait for the contract, then follow it.
                 *
                 * `window.urcAuth` is published by a deferred module and this script runs during
                 * parse, so it is reliably absent on the first call above. Two ways in, and both are
                 * needed:
                 *
                 * - `urc-auth:changed`, dispatched on `window` by the auth bundle from inside its
                 *   own `store.subscribe`. This is the path that fires on an ordinary page, and the
                 *   bundle dispatches it for exactly this kind of consumer -- one that must not
                 *   import from it (D-055).
                 * - a direct `subscribe()` if the contract happens to already be there, which is
                 *   the bfcache-restore case and anything that re-runs this block.
                 *
                 * The event carries the state in `detail`, and this deliberately ignores it and
                 * re-reads through `state()` instead: one source, so the two cannot disagree, and no
                 * dependence on an event shape a listener does not own.
                 */
                window.addEventListener('urc-auth:changed', apply);

                if (window.urcAuth && typeof window.urcAuth.subscribe === 'function') {
                    window.urcAuth.subscribe(apply);
                }
            })();
            
    // Prevent home-hero splide visual break during initialization
    // ONLY affects .home-hero-container to not interfere with category sliders
    (function() {
        function initHomeHeroSplide() {
            // Only target the home-hero-container specifically
            const container = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container.splide');
            if (!container) return;

            // Only target elements directly inside match-section
            const matchSection = container.querySelector('.match-section');
            if (!matchSection) return;

            const track = matchSection.querySelector(':scope > .splide__track');
            const list = track ? track.querySelector(':scope > .splide__list') : null;
            const slides = list ? list.querySelectorAll(':scope > .splide__slide') : [];

            if (track && list && slides.length > 0) {
                // Set track overflow hidden to prevent slides from showing outside
                track.style.overflow = 'hidden';
                track.style.position = 'relative';

                // Configure list
                list.style.display = 'flex';
                list.style.position = 'relative';
                list.style.width = '100%';

                // Hide all slides except the first one
                slides.forEach((slide, index) => {
                    if (index === 0) {
                        // Show first slide
                        slide.style.position = 'relative';
                        slide.style.opacity = '1';
                        slide.style.visibility = 'visible';
                        slide.style.pointerEvents = 'auto';
                    } else {
                        // Hide other slides
                        slide.style.position = 'absolute';
                        slide.style.left = '0';
                        slide.style.top = '0';
                        slide.style.width = '100%';
                        slide.style.opacity = '0';
                        slide.style.visibility = 'hidden';
                        slide.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
                    }
                });
            }
        }

        // Run immediately
        initHomeHeroSplide();

        // Also run on DOMContentLoaded as backup
        if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
            document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', initHomeHeroSplide);
        }

        // bfcache: re-apply pre-init slide hiding if Splide has not mounted yet after restore
        window.addEventListener('pageshow', function(event) {
            if (event.persisted) {
                initHomeHeroSplide();
            }
        });
    })();

            // Import Firebase SDK (modular)
            import { initializeApp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/12.2.1/firebase-app.js";
            import { getDatabase, ref, onChildAdded, onChildChanged, onValue, get } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/12.2.1/firebase-database.js";

            // Firebase configuration (from PHP env)
            const firebaseConfig = {
                apiKey: "AIzaSyDctv2PzSMiEMnk66rC8ZoxGHFygbj3LQI",
                authDomain: "urc-data-push-notifier.firebaseapp.com",
                databaseURL: "https://urc-data-push-notifier-default-rtdb.europe-west1.firebasedatabase.app",
                projectId: "urc-data-push-notifier",
                storageBucket: "urc-data-push-notifier.firebasestorage.app",
                messagingSenderId: "739085063936",
                appId: "1:739085063936:web:29a3f648bbf2130a83c5e5",
                measurementId: "G-ZSCBKTHN6V"
            };

            // Initialize Firebase
            const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
            const db = getDatabase(app);

            /**
             * FirebaseScoreUpdater
             * - listens for matchId children under live_data_dumps
             * - attaches per-match listener to live_data_dumps/{matchId}/matchstats
             * - updates DOM fixture-cards and home-hero using same functions & names as requested
             * - handles template switching from post-match to live-match
             * - initializes Splide for multiple live matches
             */
            class FirebaseScoreUpdater {
                constructor() {
                    this.updateQueue = new Set();
                    this.updateInProgress = false;
                    this.retryCount = {};
                    this.initialLoad = true;
                    this.initialMatchesProcessed = new Set();
                    this.activeMatchListeners = new Map(); // matchId -> unsubscribe function (if provided)
                    this.currentHeroState = null; // Track current hero state (fixture/in-play/results)
                    this.liveMatchesCache = new Map(); // Cache live match data for hero updates (matchId -> {scoreData, lastUpdated})
                    this.currentHeroMatchId = null; // Track which match ID is currently displayed in home-hero
                    this.matchToShow = null; // Track which match should be shown (from Firebase push)
                    this.debugMode = true; // Debug logging enabled to diagnose live match issues
                    this.templateSwitchInProgress = false; // Prevent multiple simultaneous template switches
                    this.templateSwitchTimeout = null; // No longer used - removed debouncing
                    this.lastTemplateSwitchTime = 0; // No longer used - removed time-based checks
                    this.splideInitializing = false; // Prevent Splide re-initialization during active init
                    this.checkHeroTemplateSwitchInProgress = false; // Prevent recursive calls to checkHeroTemplateSwitch
                    this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch = new Set(); // Track matches that have already triggered a template switch (prevent duplicate switches)
                    this.init();
                }

                log(message, data = null) {
                    // Logging disabled to reduce console noise
                    return;
                }

                init() {
                    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => {
                        const fixtureCards = document.querySelectorAll('.fixture-card[data-match-id]');
                        fixtureCards.forEach(card => {
                            const matchId = card.dataset.matchId;
                            if (matchId) {
                                this.initialMatchesProcessed.add(String(matchId));
                            }
                        });
                        
                        this.detectCurrentHeroState();
                        this.detectCurrentHeroMatch(); // Detect initial match ID
                    });

                    this.setupFirebaseListeners();

                    // Back-forward cache restore: Splide keeps stale transforms/slide widths; refresh layout
                    window.addEventListener('pageshow', (e) => {
                        if (e.persisted) {
                            this.refreshHomeHeroSplideAfterBfCache();
                        }
                    });
                    
                    // Mark initial load as complete immediately - no delay needed
                    // Removed setTimeout to prevent delays
                    this.initialLoad = false;
                    
                    // Periodic check to ensure template state stays correct
                    // Note: This is for ongoing monitoring, not initial page load
                    setInterval(() => {
                        // Clean up completed matches from cache
                        this.cleanupCompletedMatches();
                        // Reconcile fixture cards when a final non-live event is missed.
                        this.reconcileLiveFixtureCards();
                        this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                    }, 2000); // Check every 2 seconds
                }

                /**
                 * Detect current hero state from DOM classes
                 */
                detectCurrentHeroState() {
                    const matchSection = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container .match-section');
                    
                    if (!matchSection) {
                        // this.log('⚠️ No .match-section found in hero');
                        return;
                    }
                    
                    if (matchSection.classList.contains('live-match-bg')) {
                        this.currentHeroState = 'in-play';
                    } else if (matchSection.classList.contains('post-match-bg')) {
                        const hasLiveMatch = document.querySelector('.live-match-container');
                        this.currentHeroState = hasLiveMatch ? 'in-play' : 'fixture';
                    } else {
                        this.currentHeroState = 'fixture';
                    }
                    
                    // this.log(`🎯 Current hero state detected: ${this.currentHeroState}`, {
                    //     hasLiveMatchBg: matchSection.classList.contains('live-match-bg'),
                    //     hasPostMatchBg: matchSection.classList.contains('post-match-bg'),
                    //     hasLiveMatchContainer: !!document.querySelector('.live-match-container')
                    // });
                }

                /**
                 * setupFirebaseListeners
                 * Listen to live_data_dumps for new matchId children and attach per-match listeners
                 */
                // setupFirebaseListeners() {
                //     // this.log('🔌 Setting up Firebase listeners');
                    
                //     try {
                //         const dumpsRef = ref(db, 'live_data_dumps');

                //         // When a new matchId is added under live_data_dumps
                //         onChildAdded(dumpsRef, (childSnap) => {
                //             const matchId = childSnap.key;
                //             if (!matchId) return;
                //             // this.log(`➕ New match child added: ${matchId}`);
                //             // Attach per-match listener for matchstats
                //             this.ensureMatchListener(matchId);
                //         });

                //         // Also handle structured updates to live_data_dumps (initial snapshot + updates)
                //         onValue(dumpsRef, (snapshot) => {
                //             const current = snapshot.exists() ? snapshot.val() : {};
                //             const matchIds = Object.keys(current || {});
                //             // this.log(`📊 Firebase snapshot received, ${matchIds.length} matches`, matchIds);
                //             matchIds.forEach(id => this.ensureMatchListener(id));
                //         });
                //     } catch (err) {
                //         this.log('❌ Error setting up Firebase listeners', err);
                //     }
                // }

                setupFirebaseListeners() {
                    const dumpsRef = ref(db, 'live_data_dumps');

                    // Remove previous listeners if any
                    if (this.mainUnsubscribe) {
                        this.mainUnsubscribe();
                    }

                    // Use only ONE listener to avoid double triggering
                    this.mainUnsubscribe = onValue(dumpsRef, (snap) => {

                        if (!snap.exists()) return;

                        const data = snap.val();
                        const matchIds = Object.keys(data);

                        matchIds.forEach(id => this.ensureMatchListener(id));
                    });
                }

                /**
                 * Attach per-match listener for matchstats if not already attached
                 * Path: live_data_dumps/{matchId}/matchstats
                 */
                ensureMatchListener(matchId) {
                    if (!matchId) return;
                    const id = String(matchId);

                    if (this.activeMatchListeners.has(id)) {
                        return; // already listening
                    }


                    const statsRef = ref(db, `live_data_dumps/${id}/matchstats`);

                    // Use onValue to get the whole matchstats node whenever it changes
                    const unsubscribe = onValue(statsRef, (snapshot) => {
                        const payload = snapshot.exists() ? snapshot.val() : null;
                        // Provide data directly to handler
                        this.handleMatchUpdate(id, 'CHANGED', payload);
                    });

                    // store the unsubscribe (Firebase modular onValue returns an unsubscribe function)
                    this.activeMatchListeners.set(id, unsubscribe);
                }

                /**
                 * handleMatchUpdate(matchId, source, matchData)
                 * If matchData provided, process immediately; otherwise queue for fetching.
                 */
                handleMatchUpdate(matchId, source = 'unknown', matchData = null) {
                    if (!matchId) return;

                    if (matchData) {
                        const scoreData = this.extractScoreData(matchData);
                        
                        // this.log(`🔄 Processing match ${matchId}`, {
                        //     isLive: scoreData.isLive,
                        //     isCompleted: scoreData.isCompleted,
                        //     period: scoreData.period,
                        //     matchStatus: scoreData.matchStatus,
                        //     homeScore: scoreData.home,
                        //     awayScore: scoreData.away
                        // });
                        
                        // Cache live match data with timestamp
                        const wasLive = this.liveMatchesCache.has(matchId);
                        const now = Date.now();
                        
                        if (scoreData.isLive) {
                            // this.log(`🟢 Match ${matchId} is LIVE`);
                            // Store with timestamp to track most recently updated
                            this.liveMatchesCache.set(matchId, {
                                ...scoreData,
                                lastUpdated: now
                            });
                            
                            // If this match just went live, switch to it (but only once per match)
                            if (!wasLive) {
                            const matchIdStr = String(matchId);
                                
                                // Only trigger switch if we haven't already triggered it for this match
                            if (!this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.has(matchIdStr)) {
                                    this.matchToShow = matchId; // Mark this match to be shown
                                    this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.add(matchIdStr); // Mark that we've triggered switch for this match
                                    // Switch immediately - no debouncing, no delays
                                    // This prevents flicker by switching instantly
                                    this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                                }
                                // If we already triggered a switch for this match, just update the cache
                                // Don't trigger another switch - this prevents multiple reloads
                            } else {
                                // This match was already live - only switch if:
                                // 1. It's a different match AND
                                // 2. We haven't switched recently (debounce) AND
                                // 3. No template switch is in progress AND
                                // 4. We haven't already triggered a switch for this match
                                const currentMatchId = this.detectCurrentHeroMatch();
                                const matchIdStr = String(matchId);
                                
                                if (currentMatchId !== matchId && 
                                    !this.templateSwitchInProgress &&
                                    !this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.has(matchIdStr)) {
                                    // this.log(`🔄 Match ${matchId} received Firebase push, will switch from ${currentMatchId} immediately`);
                                    this.matchToShow = matchId; // Mark this match to be shown
                                    this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.add(matchIdStr); // Mark that we've triggered switch for this match
                                    // Switch immediately - no debouncing, no delays
                                    this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                                }
                            }
                        } else {
                            if (wasLive) {
                                this.liveMatchesCache.delete(matchId);
                                // Clear the "triggered switch" flag when match ends
                                this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.delete(String(matchId));
                                // this.log(`❌ Match ${matchId} completed, removed from live cache`);
                                
                                // Check remaining live matches count
                                const remainingLiveMatches = this.getActuallyLiveMatches();
                                const remainingCount = remainingLiveMatches.length;
                                
                                // If the currently displayed match completed, clear it
                                if (this.currentHeroMatchId === matchId) {
                                    // this.log(`⚠️ Currently displayed match ${matchId} completed, will trigger template switch`);
                                    this.currentHeroMatchId = null;
                                }
                                
                                // If we went from 2+ matches to 1 match, refresh template to remove completed match
                                const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                                if (homeHeroContainer && homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide') && remainingCount === 1) {
                                    // Force template refresh to show only the remaining live match
                                    this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                                    return; // Don't call checkHeroTemplateSwitch again
                                }
                                
                                // Check if we need to switch templates
                                this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                            }
                        }
                        
                        this.updateFixtureCards({ [matchId]: scoreData });
                        
                        // REMOVED: Duplicate template switch call that was causing multiple reloads
                        // The template switch is already handled above when match goes live (!wasLive)
                        // No need to call it again here
                        return;
                    }

                    // If no matchData provided, queue for processing
                    this.updateQueue.add(matchId);

                    if (this.updateInProgress) return;

                        this.updateInProgress = true;
                    // Process immediately - no delays
                    this.processUpdates();
                }

                /**
                 * processUpdates()
                 * Fetch matchstats for queued matchIds (if per-match listener did not supply data)
                 */
                async processUpdates() {
                    try {
                        const matchesToUpdate = Array.from(this.updateQueue);
                    this.updateQueue.clear();

                        if (matchesToUpdate.length === 0) {
                        this.updateInProgress = false;
                        return;
                    }

                        const scores = {};

                        for (const matchId of matchesToUpdate) {
                            try {
                                const statsRef = ref(db, `live_data_dumps/${matchId}/matchstats`);
                                const snapshot = await get(statsRef);
                                if (snapshot.exists()) {
                                    const matchData = snapshot.val();
                                    const scoreData = this.extractScoreData(matchData);
                                    scores[matchId] = scoreData;
                                    
                                    // Cache live match data with timestamp
                                    const now = Date.now();
                                    const wasLive = this.liveMatchesCache.has(matchId);
                                    
                                    if (scoreData.isLive) {
                                        this.liveMatchesCache.set(matchId, {
                                            ...scoreData,
                                            lastUpdated: now
                                        });
                                        
                                        // If match just went live and we haven't triggered switch yet
                                        if (!wasLive) {
                                            const matchIdStr = String(matchId);
                                    if (!this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.has(matchIdStr)) {
                                                this.matchToShow = matchId;
                                        this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.add(matchIdStr);
                                                // Trigger template switch immediately - no debouncing
                                                this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                                            }
                                        }
                                    } else {
                                        if (wasLive) {
                                            this.liveMatchesCache.delete(matchId);
                                            // Clear the "triggered switch" flag when match ends
                                            this.matchesThatTriggeredSwitch.delete(String(matchId));
                                            // this.log(`❌ Match ${matchId} completed, removed from live cache`);
                                            
                                            // Check remaining live matches count
                                            const remainingLiveMatches = this.getActuallyLiveMatches();
                                            const remainingCount = remainingLiveMatches.length;
                                            
                                            // If the currently displayed match completed, clear it
                                            if (this.currentHeroMatchId === matchId) {
                                                // this.log(`⚠️ Currently displayed match ${matchId} completed, will trigger template switch`);
                                                this.currentHeroMatchId = null;
                                            }
                                            
                                            // If we went from 2+ matches to 1 match, refresh template to remove completed match
                                            const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                                            if (homeHeroContainer && homeHeroContainer.classList.contains('splide') && remainingCount === 1) {
                                                // Force template refresh to show only the remaining live match
                                                this.switchHeroToLiveTemplate();
                                                return; // Don't call checkHeroTemplateSwitch again
                                            }
                                            
                                            // Check if we need to switch templates
                                            this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();
                                        }
                                    }
                                } else {
                                    // skip silently if no data
                            }
                        } catch (err) {
                                // increment retry count (no console)
                                this.retryCount[matchId] = (this.retryCount[matchId] || 0) + 1;
                        }
                    }

                        this.updateFixtureCards(scores);
                        
                        // Check if hero template switch is needed
                    this.checkHeroTemplateSwitch();

                    } catch (err) {
                        // silent error handling
                    } finally {
                        this.updateInProgress = false;
                    if (this.updateQueue.size > 0) {
                            // Process immediately - no delays
                            this.processUpdates();
                        }
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * extractScoreData(matchData)
                 * Normalizes many possible shapes into a { home, away, isLive, ... } object
                 */
                extractScoreData(matchData) {
                    let home = null, away = null, period = 'pre', matchStatus = null;

                    if (!matchData || typeof matchData !== 'object') {
                        return {
                            home: null,
                            away: null,
                            isLive: false,
                            period: 'pre',
                            matchStatus: null,
                            hasScoreData: false,
                            isCompleted: false
                        };
                    }

                    if (matchData.matchPeriod || matchData.last_updated || matchData.homeTeam || matchData.awayTeam) {
                        const rawPeriod = matchData.matchPeriod || matchData.period || 'pre';
                        // Normalize "pre match" to "pre" since they mean the same thing
                        // NOTE: This normalization ONLY affects "pre match" -> "pre". Other periods like "post", "in-play", "live" remain unchanged.
                        period = (String(rawPeriod).toLowerCase() === 'pre match' || String(rawPeriod).toLowerCase() === 'pre-match') ? 'pre' : rawPeriod;
                        matchStatus = matchData.matchStatus || null;
                        home = matchData.homeTeam?.score?.currentScore ?? matchData.homeTeam?.score ?? matchData.homeScore ?? matchData.home_score ?? matchData.home ?? null;
                        away = matchData.awayTeam?.score?.currentScore ?? matchData.awayTeam?.score ?? matchData.awayScore ?? matchData.away_score ?? matchData.away ?? null;
                    } else {
                        home = matchData.homeScore ?? matchData.home_score ?? matchData.home ?? null;
                        away = matchData.awayScore ?? matchData.away_score ?? matchData.away ?? null;
                        const rawPeriod = matchData.period || 'pre';
                        // Normalize "pre match" to "pre" since they mean the same thing
                        // NOTE: This normalization ONLY affects "pre match" -> "pre". Other periods like "post", "in-play", "live" remain unchanged.
                        period = (String(rawPeriod).toLowerCase() === 'pre match' || String(rawPeriod).toLowerCase() === 'pre-match') ? 'pre' : rawPeriod;
                        matchStatus = matchData.matchStatus || null;
                    }

                    const parseOrNull = (v) => (v !== null && v !== undefined && v !== '') ? parseInt(v, 10) : null;

                    const hasScores = (home !== null && home !== undefined) || (away !== null && away !== undefined);
                    const homeNum = parseOrNull(home);
                    const awayNum = parseOrNull(away);

                    const periodStr = String(period ?? '').toLowerCase().trim();
                    const periodCompact = periodStr.replace(/\s+/g, '');
                    const statusStr = String(matchStatus ?? '').toLowerCase().trim();
                    const statusCompact = statusStr.replace(/\s+/g, '');

                    // Check if match is completed FIRST - this takes priority
                    // Note: "pre match" should NEVER be treated as completed, regardless of scores
                    // APIs often send "Full Time", "full time", or "fulltime" — treat all as finished.
                    const isCompleted = (
                        periodStr === 'post' ||
                        periodStr === 'result' ||
                        periodStr === 'full time' ||
                        periodStr === 'full-time' ||
                        periodCompact === 'fulltime' ||
                        periodStr === 'ft' ||
                        statusStr === 'result' ||
                        statusStr === 'completed' ||
                        statusStr === 'full-time' ||
                        statusStr === 'full time' ||
                        statusCompact === 'fulltime' ||
                        statusStr === 'ft' ||
                        statusStr === 'ended'
                    );

                    // A match is NOT live if it's completed, regardless of other indicators
                    // IMPORTANT: "in-play" matchStatus means the match is live (checked below)
                    // IMPORTANT: "post" period means the match is over (checked in isCompleted above)
                    const livePeriods = ['live', 'first half', 'second half', 'extra time', 'in-play'];
                    const isLive = !isCompleted && (
                        livePeriods.includes(periodStr) ||
                        statusStr === 'in-play' ||
                        statusStr === 'live'
                    );

                    return {
                        home: homeNum,
                        away: awayNum,
                        isLive: !!isLive,
                        period: period,
                        matchStatus: matchStatus,
                        hasScoreData: !!hasScores,
                        isCompleted: !!isCompleted
                    };
                }

                /**
                 * updateFixtureCards(scores)
                 * scores: { matchId: scoreData, ... }
                 */
                updateFixtureCards(scores) {
                    if (!scores || Object.keys(scores).length === 0) return;

                    let hasUpdates = false;

                    Object.entries(scores).forEach(([matchId, scoreData]) => {
                        const fixtureCard = document.querySelector(`.fixture-card[data-match-id="${matchId}"]`);
                        if (fixtureCard) {
                            fixtureCard.setAttribute('data-firebase-seen-at', String(Date.now()));
                            const hasTeamScores = fixtureCard.querySelector('.team-scores');
                            const hasTeamInfo = fixtureCard.querySelector('.team-info');

                            // On initial load, don't modify cards that already have correct COMPLETED state from PHP
                            // But ALWAYS allow live matches to update (they need real-time score updates)
                            if (this.initialLoad && hasTeamScores && !scoreData.isLive) {
                                // Check if the card is already in the correct completed state
                                const isCardInPostState = fixtureCard.classList.contains('fixture-post');
                                if (isCardInPostState && scoreData.isCompleted) {
                                    // Card already correctly rendered as completed by PHP, skip modification
                                    return;
                                }
                            }

                            // Don't change to pre if card already shows scores
                            // BUT: Always process completed matches to ensure scores are shown
                            const cardWasLive = fixtureCard.classList.contains('fixture-live');
                            if (hasTeamScores && !scoreData.isLive && !scoreData.isCompleted && !cardWasLive) {
                                // keep current state
                                return;
                            }

                            const homeScoreEl = fixtureCard.querySelector('.score[data-team="home"]');
                            const awayScoreEl = fixtureCard.querySelector('.score[data-team="away"]');

                            if (homeScoreEl && (scoreData.home !== null && scoreData.home !== undefined)) {
                                const currentHomeScore = (homeScoreEl.textContent || '').trim();
                                const newHomeScore = String(scoreData.home ?? '');
                                
                                // this.log(`📊 Match ${matchId} - Home score check:`, {
                                //     current: currentHomeScore,
                                //     new: newHomeScore,
                                //     willUpdate: currentHomeScore !== newHomeScore && !(newHomeScore === '0' && currentHomeScore !== '0')
                                // });
                                
                                if (currentHomeScore !== newHomeScore && !(newHomeScore === '0' && currentHomeScore !== '0')) {
                                    homeScoreEl.textContent = newHomeScore;
                                hasUpdates = true;
                                    // this.log(`✅ Updated home score for match ${matchId}: ${currentHomeScore} → ${newHomeScore}`);
                                }
                            } else if (scoreData.isCompleted && scoreData.home !== null && scoreData.home !== undefined) {
                                // If match is completed but score element doesn't exist yet, updateLiveStatus will create it
                                hasUpdates = true;
                            }

                            if (awayScoreEl && (scoreData.away !== null && scoreData.away !== undefined)) {
                                const currentAwayScore = (awayScoreEl.textContent || '').trim();
                                const newAwayScore = String(scoreData.away ?? '');
                                
                                // this.log(`📊 Match ${matchId} - Away score check:`, {
                                //     current: currentAwayScore,
                                //     new: newAwayScore,
                                //     willUpdate: currentAwayScore !== newAwayScore && !(newAwayScore === '0' && currentAwayScore !== '0')
                                // });
                                
                                if (currentAwayScore !== newAwayScore && !(newAwayScore === '0' && currentAwayScore !== '0')) {
                                    awayScoreEl.textContent = newAwayScore;
                                hasUpdates = true;
                                    // this.log(`✅ Updated away score for match ${matchId}: ${currentAwayScore} → ${newAwayScore}`);
                                }
                            } else if (scoreData.isCompleted && scoreData.away !== null && scoreData.away !== undefined) {
                                // If match is completed but score element doesn't exist yet, updateLiveStatus will create it
                                hasUpdates = true;
                            }

                            // Update live / status UI
                            this.updateLiveStatus(fixtureCard, scoreData.isLive, scoreData);
                        }

                        // Update home-hero if present
                        this.updateHomeHeroMatch(matchId, scoreData);
                        this.updateHomeHeroState(matchId, scoreData);
                    });

                    if (hasUpdates) {
                        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('firebaseScoreUpdated', { detail: { scores } }));
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * updateLiveStatus(card, isLive, scoreData)
                 */
                updateLiveStatus(card, isLive, scoreData = null) {
                    if (!card) return;
                    const statusContainer = card.querySelector('.match-status-indicator');
                    const hasTeamScores = card.querySelector('.team-scores');
                    const hasTeamInfo = card.querySelector('.team-info');

                    // Don't modify cards that already have the correct completed state from PHP
                    // But ALWAYS allow live matches to update (they need real-time updates)
                    if (this.initialLoad && hasTeamScores && !isLive && scoreData?.isCompleted) {
                        const isCardInPostState = card.classList.contains('fixture-post');
                        if (isCardInPostState) {
                            // Card already rendered as completed by PHP, don't modify
                            return;
                        }
                    }

                    // Allow status-only transitions (e.g. live -> full-time) even if score payload is missing.
                    if (hasTeamScores && !isLive && !scoreData?.hasScoreData && !scoreData?.isCompleted) {
                        return;
                    }

                    if (isLive) {
                        card.classList.add('fixture-live');
                        card.classList.remove('fixture-pre', 'fixture-post');
                        card.removeAttribute('data-fulltime-at');

                        if (!hasTeamScores && hasTeamInfo) {
                            const homeNameEl = card.querySelector('.team-name.home-name');
                            const awayNameEl = card.querySelector('.team-name.away-name');
                            const homeLogoEl = card.querySelector('.team-logo.home-logo');
                            const awayLogoEl = card.querySelector('.team-logo.away-logo');

                            if (homeNameEl && awayNameEl) {
                                const teamScoresHTML = `
                                    <div class="team-scores" style="display:flex !important;">
                                        <div class="team-side">
                                            ${homeLogoEl ? `<img src="${homeLogoEl.src}" alt="${homeNameEl.textContent}" class="team-logo">` : `<div class="team-acronym">${homeNameEl.textContent}</div>`}
                                            <div class="score" data-team="home">${scoreData?.home ?? 0}</div>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="match-status-indicator">
                                            <div class="live-indicator">LIVE</div>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="team-side">
                                            <div class="score" data-team="away">${scoreData?.away ?? 0}</div>
                                            ${awayLogoEl ? `<img src="${awayLogoEl.src}" alt="${awayNameEl.textContent}" class="team-logo">` : `<div class="team-acronym">${awayNameEl.textContent}</div>`}
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                `;
                                hasTeamInfo.outerHTML = teamScoresHTML;
                                const dt = card.querySelector('.match-date-time');
                                if (dt) dt.style.display = 'none';
                            }
                        } else if (hasTeamScores) {
                            hasTeamScores.style.display = 'block';
                            if (hasTeamInfo) hasTeamInfo.style.display = 'none';
                            const dt = card.querySelector('.match-date-time');
                            if (dt) dt.style.display = 'none';
                        }

                        if (statusContainer) statusContainer.innerHTML = '<div class="live-indicator">LIVE</div>';
                        this.updateMatchActions(card, 'live');
                        // Live/in-play: only FOLLOW LIVE — hide stadium name
                        const venueEl = card.querySelector('.match-venue');
                        if (venueEl) venueEl.style.display = 'none';
                        return;
                    }

                    const cardWasLive = card.classList.contains('fixture-live');
                    card.classList.remove('fixture-live');

                    let isPost = false;
                    if (scoreData) {
                        const hasScores = scoreData.home !== null || scoreData.away !== null;
                        const periodLower = String(scoreData.period || '').toLowerCase().trim();
                        const periodCompact = periodLower.replace(/\s+/g, '');
                        const statusLower = String(scoreData.matchStatus || '').toLowerCase().trim();
                        const statusCompact = statusLower.replace(/\s+/g, '');
                        // IMPORTANT: "pre" or "pre match" should NEVER be treated as completed, regardless of scores
                        const prePeriods = ['pre', 'pre match', 'pre-match'];
                        if (prePeriods.includes(periodLower)) {
                            isPost = false;
                        } else {
                            // Prefer upstream normalized completion when available.
                            // Fallback keeps full-time variants resilient across data providers.
                            const isCompleted = !!scoreData.isCompleted || (
                                periodLower === 'post' ||
                                periodLower === 'result' ||
                                periodLower === 'full time' ||
                                periodLower === 'full-time' ||
                                periodCompact === 'fulltime' ||
                                periodLower === 'ft' ||
                                statusLower === 'result' ||
                                statusLower === 'completed' ||
                                statusLower === 'full time' ||
                                statusLower === 'full-time' ||
                                statusCompact === 'fulltime' ||
                                statusLower === 'ft' ||
                                statusLower === 'ended'
                            );
                            isPost = !!isCompleted;
                            // Fallback: if card was live and feed moved out of live without explicit completed flags,
                            // treat as post so UI can show FT and then hide after grace period.
                            if (!isPost && cardWasLive && !isLive) {
                                isPost = true;
                            }
                        }
                    } else {
                        const hasScores = card.querySelector('.score[data-team="home"]') && card.querySelector('.score[data-team="away"]');
                        const hasLiveIndicator = card.querySelector('.live-indicator');
                        isPost = !!(hasScores && !hasLiveIndicator);
                    }

                        const wasPost = card.classList.contains('fixture-post');
                        card.classList.toggle('fixture-post', isPost);
                        card.classList.toggle('fixture-pre', !isPost);
                        if (!wasPost && isPost) {
                            card.setAttribute('data-fulltime-at', String(Date.now()));
                        } else if (wasPost && !isPost) {
                            card.removeAttribute('data-fulltime-at');
                        }
                        
                    // Ensure team-scores section is visible for completed matches
                    if (isPost) {
                        const hasTeamScores = card.querySelector('.team-scores');
                        const hasTeamInfo = card.querySelector('.team-info');
                        
                        if (!hasTeamScores && hasTeamInfo) {
                            // Extract team names from team-info section
                            const homeNameEl = card.querySelector('.team-name.home-name');
                            const awayNameEl = card.querySelector('.team-name.away-name');
                            const homeLogoEl = card.querySelector('.team-logo.home-logo');
                            const awayLogoEl = card.querySelector('.team-logo.away-logo');
                            
                            if (homeNameEl && awayNameEl) {
                                // Create team scores section with proper structure matching the PHP template
                                const teamScoresHTML = `
                                    <div class="team-scores" style="display: flex !important;">
                                        <div class="team-side">
                                            ${homeLogoEl ? `<img src="${homeLogoEl.src}" alt="${homeNameEl.textContent}" class="team-logo">` : `<div class="team-acronym">${homeNameEl.textContent}</div>`}
                                            <div class="score" data-team="home">${scoreData?.home ?? 0}</div>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="match-status-indicator">
                                            <div class="fulltime-indicator"><span class="full-text">FULL TIME</span><span class="short-text">FT</span></div>
                                        </div>
                                        <div class="team-side">
                                            <div class="score" data-team="away">${scoreData?.away ?? 0}</div>
                                            ${awayLogoEl ? `<img src="${awayLogoEl.src}" alt="${awayNameEl.textContent}" class="team-logo">` : `<div class="team-acronym">${awayNameEl.textContent}</div>`}
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                `;
                                
                                // Replace team-info with team-scores
                                hasTeamInfo.outerHTML = teamScoresHTML;
                                
                                // Hide date-time section
                                const dt = card.querySelector('.match-date-time');
                                if (dt) dt.style.display = 'none';
                            }
                        } else if (hasTeamScores) {
                            // Ensure team-scores section is visible
                            hasTeamScores.style.display = 'block';
                            
                            // Hide team info section if it exists
                            if (hasTeamInfo) {
                                hasTeamInfo.style.display = 'none';
                            }
                            
                            // Hide date-time section for completed matches
                            const dt = card.querySelector('.match-date-time');
                            if (dt) dt.style.display = 'none';
                        }
                    }

                    if (statusContainer) {
                        statusContainer.innerHTML = isPost ? '<div class="fulltime-indicator"><span class="full-text">FULL TIME</span><span class="short-text">FT</span></div>' : '';
                    }

                    this.updateMatchActions(card, isPost ? 'completed' : 'pre');
                    // Stadium name only for pre-match; hide for post (result) and live (in-play)
                    const venueEl = card.querySelector('.match-venue');
                    if (venueEl) venueEl.style.display = isPost ? 'none' : '';
                }

                /**
                 * updateMatchActions(card, state)
                 */
                updateMatchActions(card, state) {
                    if (!card) return;
                    let matchActionsContainer = card.querySelector('.match-actions');
                    const dateTimeEl = card.querySelector('.match-date-time');

                    if (state === 'live') {
                        if (!matchActionsContainer) {
                            matchActionsContainer = document.createElement('div');
                            matchActionsContainer.className = 'match-actions';
                            const insertAfter = card.querySelector('.team-scores') || card.querySelector('.team-info');
                            if (insertAfter && insertAfter.parentNode) {
                                insertAfter.parentNode.insertBefore(matchActionsContainer, insertAfter.nextSibling);
                            } else {
                                card.appendChild(matchActionsContainer);
                            }
                        }
                        matchActionsContainer.innerHTML = `
                            <span class="dot"></span>
                            <span class="action-button follow-live">FOLLOW LIVE</span>
                        `;
                        if (dateTimeEl) dateTimeEl.style.display = 'none';
                    } else if (state === 'completed') {
                        if (!matchActionsContainer) {
                            matchActionsContainer = document.createElement('div');
                            matchActionsContainer.className = 'match-actions';
                            const insertAfter = card.querySelector('.team-scores') || card.querySelector('.team-info');
                            if (insertAfter && insertAfter.parentNode) {
                                insertAfter.parentNode.insertBefore(matchActionsContainer, insertAfter.nextSibling);
                            } else {
                                card.appendChild(matchActionsContainer);
                            }
                        }
                        matchActionsContainer.innerHTML = `
                            <span class="icon-recap"></span>
                            <span class="action-button recap">Recap</span>
                        `;
                        if (dateTimeEl) dateTimeEl.style.display = 'none';
                    } else {
                        if (matchActionsContainer) matchActionsContainer.remove();
                        if (dateTimeEl) dateTimeEl.style.display = '';
                    }
                }

                /**
                 * updateHomeHeroMatch(matchId, scoreData)
                 */
                updateHomeHeroMatch(matchId, scoreData) {
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) return;

                    const homeHeroMatch = homeHeroContainer.querySelector(`#match-${matchId}`);
                    if (!homeHeroMatch) {
                        if (scoreData.isLive) {
                            this.updateHomeHeroToShowLiveMatch(matchId, scoreData);
                        }
                        return;
                    }

                    const liveBtn = homeHeroMatch.querySelector('.live-btn');
                    if (liveBtn) {
                        if (scoreData.isLive) {
                            liveBtn.textContent = 'LIVE';
                            liveBtn.style.backgroundColor = '#ff4b5a';
                            liveBtn.style.color = '#ffffff';
                        } else if (scoreData.isCompleted) {
                            liveBtn.textContent = 'FULL TIME';
                            liveBtn.style.backgroundColor = '#00bcd4';
                            liveBtn.style.color = '#ffffff';
                        } else {
                            liveBtn.textContent = 'FIXTURE';
                            liveBtn.style.backgroundColor = '#666666';
                            liveBtn.style.color = '#ffffff';
                        }
                    }

                    const liveIndicators = homeHeroMatch.querySelectorAll('.live-indicator');
                    liveIndicators.forEach(indicator => {
                    if (scoreData.isLive) {
                            indicator.textContent = 'LIVE';
                            indicator.style.display = 'block';
                    } else if (scoreData.isCompleted) {
                            indicator.className = 'fulltime-indicator';
                            indicator.innerHTML = '<span class="full-text">FULL TIME</span><span class="short-text">FT</span>';
                            indicator.style.display = 'block';
                        } else {
                            indicator.style.display = 'none';
                        }
                    });
                }

                /**
                 * updateHomeHeroToShowLiveMatch(matchId, scoreData)
                 */
                updateHomeHeroToShowLiveMatch(matchId, scoreData) {
                    const homeHeroContainer = document.querySelector('.home-hero-container');
                    if (!homeHeroContainer) return;

                    const matchSection = homeHeroContainer.querySelector('.match-section');
                    if (matchSection) {
                        matchSection.classList.remove('post-match-bg');
                        matchSection.classList.add('live-match-bg');
                    }

                    const allLiveBtns = homeHeroContainer.querySelectorAll('.live-btn');
                    allLiveBtns.forEach(btn => {
                        btn.textContent = 'LIVE';
                        btn.style.backgroundColor = '#ff4b5a';
                        btn.style.color = '#ffffff';
                    });
